====================================================================================================
SEER  -- Selective stock forecasting, cut-off 2026-03-25
====================================================================================================
Paper reference : Figure 4(b); the trimmed version is printed as Figures 17-18 in the appendix.
Task            : Selective Stock Forecasting -- from a daily basket of 50 stocks, pick 0-6 names and
                  give each a 3-way label (Up / Neutral / Down) for the next trading day.
Cut-off date    : 2026-03-25
Context         : 70 causal-knowledge rules (numbered as SEER cites them) in the system prompt;
                  one block per stock in the user prompt with the company, the 14-trading-day close
                  history and the candidate events of the last three weeks.

Outcome         : SEER picked MU Down and AMD Down; both closed Strong Down in the next session (2/2).
                  The baseline LLM (Event RAG) picked MRK Up, AMD Up, CVX Down and XOM Down on the same
                  day; all four were wrong.

====================================================================================================
[1] SYSTEM PROMPT  (70 causal-knowledge rules)
====================================================================================================
**TASK:** From a daily basket of 50 candidate stocks, identify the **0 to 6** names you can most confidently forecast for the next 1 trading day(s) as of cutoff 2026-03-25, and produce a directional prediction only for those selected names.

**SELECTION CRITERIA:**
* Pick a stock ONLY if its Prediction Conditions reveal a dominant structural catalyst, unambiguous asymmetric signal, or clear fundamental driver.
* Skip stocks whose conditions are noisy, mixed, or lack a clear directional thesis. You are allowed to pick zero.
* Treat the daily basket holistically: account for cross-stock, sector, or macro spillovers reflected in the conditions.
* Ground your judgment in the PREDICTION KNOWLEDGE below: prefer picks whose conditions match a durable, learned rule.

**FORECAST CRITERIA (3-Way (3way)):**
For every selected stock output exactly ONE of these labels: Up / Neutral / Down.

* **"Up"**: ROC > 0.005 (Cumulative gain over 0.5%)
* **"Neutral"**: -0.005 <= ROC <= 0.005 (Flat, noise, or tight consolidation)
* **"Down"**: ROC < -0.005 (Cumulative loss worse than -0.5%)

**PREDICTION KNOWLEDGE (durable rules learned from historical batch forecasts; N/A if none):**
1. IF a stock enters an earnings/guidance catalyst after a cluster of pre-event price-target hikes, "top pick" tags, and a price series already printing fresh highs into the report THEN reduce conviction on a bullish beat-and-raise (or flip toward fade) because the positive outcome is likely priced-in and invites a sell-the-news / crowding unwind (scope: market-wide, momentum/late-cycle regime, earnings + analyst-action)
§
2. IF a stock reports a beat-and-raise while its recent event pool is dominated by bearish signals (price-target cuts, fund liquidations, prominent bear commentary) THEN raise conviction on continuation upside because the surprise resets a skeptical crowd's positioning and forces short-covering/re-rating (scope: market-wide, any regime, earnings + analyst-action)
§
3. IF a stock's bullish thesis rests on a commodity/macro price shock that has ALREADY moved the underlying and the stock has closed at a window high on the same session the catalyst broke THEN reduce conviction or fade the next-day continuation, because the initial shock is reflected in the current close and the follow-through tends to be reversion/exhaustion rather than fresh repricing (scope: energy/commodity-linked sectors, any regime, macro/geopolitical price-shock)
§
4. IF two or more of the batch's top picks derive their directional thesis from the same underlying driver (same commodity, same macro print, same sector catalyst) THEN treat them as a single correlated bet and cap combined conviction, because the apparent multi-pick confidence is illusory and one thesis reversal takes down all correlated picks simultaneously (scope: market-wide, any regime, sector/macro catalyst)
§
5. IF a pick's bullish thesis rests on a single-session outsized gap driven by a company-specific catalyst that is already fully reflected in the current close (a new customer/order win, product news, or a multi-day post-earnings recovery that has round-tripped back to fresh highs) THEN reduce conviction or fade next-day continuation, because the catalyst is spent and the parabolic session invites profit-taking/mean-reversion rather than fresh repricing (scope: market-wide, momentum/late-cycle regime, product-order or earnings-recovery catalyst)
§
6. IF the same session that produced a pick's bullish catalyst also contains an explicit profit-taking or insider/analyst sell-to-lock-gains signal (or prominent valuation-overextension commentary) in its event pool THEN treat that as a direct exhaustion tell and downgrade the pick toward Neutral/Down, because informed participants are already unwinding into the strength and the marginal buyer is thinning (scope: market-wide, any regime, catalyst-driven breakout)
§
7. IF a mega-cap raises capital-expenditure/spending guidance in the same report as a revenue beat, THEN predict Down if the report lacks concurrent proof of accelerating monetization (segment backlog growth, cloud/unit acceleration) and instead pairs the spend with margin-drag or loss-making segment commentary, but lean Up if the same report shows the spend is already converting into accelerating high-margin revenue, because the market prices rising CapEx as value-destructive absent visible ROI and value-accretive when acceleration confirms the return (scope: technology/semiconductors, momentum/late-cycle regime, earnings + guidance)
§
8. IF a stock enters earnings already at fresh highs with a pre-event price-target-hike cluster (the Rule 1 fade setup) BUT then delivers a beat whose surprise is broad-based and accompanied by explicit acceleration in a key growth segment, THEN override the priced-in fade and lean toward continuation Up, because a large positive surprise magnitude combined with fresh acceleration forces upward re-rating faster than crowded positioning can unwind (scope: market-wide, momentum/late-cycle regime, earnings + analyst-action)
§
9. IF a stock reports a headline revenue/EPS beat but its raised forward guidance falls short of the consensus bar (or the report bundles a large one-off GAAP/charge deterioration or declining secondary segments) AND the catalyst-session price action is already selling off THEN predict Down/continuation-lower, because the market prices the guidance-vs-expectations gap rather than the trailing beat and the same-session selling confirms the disappointment is being repriced, not absorbed (scope: market-wide, any regime, earnings + guidance)
§
10. IF a beat-and-raise pick's price series is already accelerating upward across the sessions immediately preceding the catalyst (higher closes into the report) rather than sitting flat or extended-then-stalling THEN raise conviction on next-day continuation Up, because a tape already voting in the thesis's direction signals demand is absorbing supply and the marginal buyer is still arriving, distinguishing a live trend from a spent priced-in move (scope: market-wide, momentum/late-cycle regime, earnings + analyst-action)
§
11. IF a stock has already fallen sharply from a recent window high into a multi-session downtrend AND the fundamental catalyst driving the decline is stale (priced days earlier) or the newest report actually contains a beat/raise, THEN do NOT extrapolate the downtrend into a next-day Down; lean Neutral/Up because extended one-directional selling with no fresh negative catalyst invites oversold mean-reversion and short-covering rather than continuation (scope: market-wide, any regime, post-earnings / analyst-action)
§
12. IF a pick delivers a genuine beat-and-raise but closes down on the catalyst session ON A DAY WHERE THE DECLINE IS ATTRIBUTABLE TO BROAD SECTOR/MARKET RISK-OFF rather than a stock-specific negative in its own event pool, THEN do NOT forecast next-day Down; lean Up because the stock-specific bullish surprise reasserts once the market-wide selling abates and the down-close was beta noise, not thesis rejection (scope: market-wide, risk-off/high-volatility regime, earnings + guidance)
§
13. IF a stock's bullish-looking catalyst cluster centers on a negotiated government/regulatory deal that grants access or removes a tariff/legal overhang in exchange for mandated price cuts or margin concessions on the core product, THEN reduce conviction or fade next-day upside, because the market prices the guaranteed hit to unit revenue/pricing power ahead of the speculative volume upside, and a headline "win" that lowers realized price is a net repricing-down event (scope: healthcare/regulated sectors, any regime, regulatory/pricing-deal catalyst)
§
14. IF a pick's directional thesis (up OR down) rests on a same-session catalyst that is narrative/commentary-based (an investigative exposé, executive rhetoric, positioning disclosures like put buys, or policy-tone signals) rather than a hard change to earnings, guidance, orders, or ratings, AND the stock has already made a large multi-session move in that direction, THEN do NOT extrapolate continuation; lean Neutral because soft catalysts do not alter forward cash-flow estimates and an already-stretched tape absorbs them as noise, mean-reverting instead (scope: market-wide, any regime, narrative/positioning catalyst)
§
15. IF a growth/momentum-priced stock reports a record or all-time-high absolute revenue/sales figure but the same report shows the year-over-year growth RATE decelerating to a multi-quarter low (and its event pool contains competing analyst commentary that a rival will overtake it), THEN reduce conviction or fade next-day continuation, because the market prices the second-derivative slowdown in growth rate rather than the record absolute level, and an extended tape treats a "record but decelerating" print as a peak-growth repricing-down tell (scope: technology/semiconductors, momentum/late-cycle regime, earnings/revenue-report)
§
16. IF a pick's bullish thesis leans on the market-wide risk-on catalyst of the day (a macro relief rally) combined with company-specific catalysts that are already several sessions stale, rather than on a fresh cutoff-dated confirming catalyst (upgrade, order, beat), THEN downgrade conviction toward Neutral, because market beta is a shared basket-wide lift that does not distinguish the pick from peers and stale catalysts are already reflected in the current close, leaving no idiosyncratic edge for next-day continuation (scope: market-wide, risk-on/relief-rally regime, macro + stale company catalyst)
§
17. IF a pick's price series is making fresh highs on a large single-session gap BUT the SAME company's own event pool contains its leadership or research desk publicly signaling broad market de-risking, correction risk, or capital rotation away from the pick's own asset class THEN fade next-day continuation toward Down, because a firm whose revenues are levered to the very market its executives are warning about is flashing a direct forward-earnings tell, and the parabolic close into that self-referential bearish signal is exhaustion rather than fresh demand (scope: financials/broker-dealers, momentum/late-cycle regime, executive-commentary/macro-warning catalyst)
§
18. IF a pick's bullish tape rests on price momentum into fresh highs while its cutoff-dated catalysts are either soft narrative or several sessions stale, with NO fresh hard confirming catalyst (upgrade, order, beat) printed on or near the cutoff, THEN downgrade toward Neutral rather than predicting continuation, because momentum without a fresh idiosyncratic driver leaves the marginal buyer unproven and an extended tape mean-reverts once the last stale catalyst is fully absorbed (scope: market-wide, momentum/late-cycle regime, stale-catalyst/momentum)
§
19. IF a pick's fresh cutoff-dated catalyst is that the pick is the ACQUIRER paying a large cash premium in an M&A deal (rather than being the acquired target) THEN downgrade toward Neutral rather than predicting continuation Up, because the acquirer outlays cash and assumes integration/pipeline-execution risk so the deal is not an earnings/revenue surprise to it — the premium accrues to the target, and the acquirer's reaction is typically muted-to-flat rather than a fresh upward repricing (scope: market-wide, any regime, M&A catalyst)
§
20. IF a pick's tape is already at fresh highs with its beat-and-raise several sessions stale AND the only fresh cutoff-dated catalyst is a non-earnings event that does not raise forward revenue/EPS expectations (an acquirer-side deal, a partnership, or pipeline optionality) THEN do NOT extrapolate the prior momentum into next-day Up; lean Neutral, because the stale beat is fully absorbed and the fresh catalyst leaves the marginal buyer unproven, inviting consolidation rather than continuation (scope: market-wide, momentum/late-cycle regime, stale-earnings + non-earnings catalyst)
§
21. IF a beaten-down stock's fresh cutoff-dated bullish catalyst is the REMOVAL of a legal/regulatory/structural tail risk (dismissed suit, blocked breakup, cleared overhang) rather than a positive change to forward revenue/EPS/orders THEN do NOT forecast next-day relief-continuation Up; lean Neutral/Down because eliminating a low-probability existential threat adds no cash-flow value and the market fades the headline relief once positioning normalizes (scope: market-wide, any regime, legal/regulatory-resolution catalyst)
§
22. IF a pick's bullish catalyst session ALSO contains fresh insider distribution signals in its own event pool (a newly adopted systematic 10b5-1 sell plan or director/officer share sales dated on or near the catalyst) THEN downgrade the pick toward Neutral/Down regardless of tape direction, because informed insiders scheduling supply into the news flag that the catalyst is not a forward-value upgrade and marginal selling pressure outweighs the relief bid (scope: market-wide, any regime, catalyst + insider-transaction)
§
23. IF a pick's bearish thesis relies on insider-sale signals that are routine pre-scheduled 10b5-1 executions (small, ratable, dated near but not triggered by the catalyst) rather than a newly ADOPTED sell plan or a discretionary sale, AND the stock is already deep in a multi-session decline, THEN do NOT treat the insider sales as a fresh exhaustion tell or forecast next-day Down; lean Neutral/Up, because pre-arranged ratable sales carry no forward-information content and layering them onto an oversold tape invites mean-reversion rather than continuation (scope: market-wide, any regime, catalyst + insider-transaction)
§
24. IF a defensive/value stock delivers a genuine beat-and-raise but the catalyst session prints an outsized single-day gap that closes at a fresh window high fully embedding the surprise, THEN reduce conviction or fade next-day continuation even when broad rotation appears to favor the name, because "defensive flows" are a shared basket-wide narrative not an idiosyncratic fresh driver, and the parabolic close on an already-known print invites next-day profit-taking rather than fresh repricing (scope: consumer/defensive sectors, risk-off/high-volatility regime, earnings + guidance)
§
25. IF a pick's outsized single-session gap is driven by a catalyst that structurally EXPANDS the company's addressable market or opens a NEW revenue stream (a supplier gaining a new large customer for a validated product, a platform monetizing a previously non-revenue asset) rather than by a self-contained one-off order/product event, THEN do NOT fade it as spent; lean toward continuation Up, because a newly opened TAM is re-rated across multiple sessions as the market digests the durable forward-earnings shift, unlike a bounded catalyst that fully embeds in one close (scope: technology/semiconductors, momentum/late-cycle regime, structural-demand/customer-win catalyst)
§
26. IF a beaten-down stock's fresh cutoff-dated bullish catalysts are exclusively analyst price-target hikes and "top pick" tags reinforcing an already-well-known thesis (no new operational fact, order, or company-reported surprise) AND the tape is a single-session snapback off a sharp multi-session decline, THEN downgrade next-day continuation toward Neutral, because derivative opinion-based catalysts on a pre-priced thesis do not supply a fresh marginal buyer and an oversold bounce into a still-broken structure consolidates rather than trends (scope: technology/semiconductors, any regime, analyst-action catalyst)
§
27. IF a pick's only fresh cutoff-dated catalyst is a media/analyst report of SECTOR ROTATION or capital flows INTO the pick's own asset class (naming it a "primary beneficiary") while its hard idiosyncratic catalysts (upgrade, trial data, order) are already several sessions stale AND the tape has run parabolically into fresh highs, THEN downgrade toward Neutral, because a rotation-flow narrative is a shared basket-wide theme that lifts all names in the group rather than an idiosyncratic forward-earnings driver, and an extended tape absorbing a stale thesis leaves no proven marginal buyer, inviting consolidation (scope: market-wide, momentum/late-cycle regime, rotation-flow/narrative catalyst)
§
28. NO_NEW_RULE
§
29. IF a pick's fresh cutoff-dated catalyst is a hard operational result that ACCUMULATES over subsequent non-trading or multi-day sessions (opening-weekend box office, launch-week unit sales, holiday-period demand data) AND the pre-catalyst tape is flat or depressed rather than having already gapped on the news, THEN lean continuation Up, because only the first fragment of the result is in the current close and the market re-rates forward expectations as each successive data point confirms the beat — unlike a self-contained one-session catalyst that fully embeds and reverts (scope: consumer/media sectors, any regime, product-launch/demand-datastream catalyst)
§
30. IF a stock's recent event pool mixes soft/mildly-negative operational commentary (attendance dip, spending caps, competitive caution) that the tape has already absorbed into a depressed base WITH a subsequent fresh hard positive result that the tape has NOT yet moved on, THEN weight the fresh unpriced hard result over the stale absorbed negatives and lean Up, because the marginal buyer responds to the newest unpriced cash-flow signal while the older soft negatives are already reflected in the beaten-down close (scope: market-wide, any regime, mixed-catalyst/demand-surprise)
§
31. IF a pick's bearish thesis rests on a negative catalyst delivered at a PRE-ANNOUNCED/scheduled event (an investor conference, a calendar-dated guidance update) that the market knew was coming AND the tape has already drifted toward window lows into that event, THEN do NOT extrapolate next-day Down; lean Neutral/Up, because the anticipated negative is pre-positioned and merely in-line-bad commentary exhausts the informed sellers, producing a sell-the-rumor/buy-the-news relief bounce rather than continued repricing (scope: market-wide, any regime, scheduled-event/guidance-commentary catalyst)
§
32. IF a pick's bearish thesis rests on a long-telegraphed structural erosion event that lands on its calendar date (a patent expiry, scheduled biosimilar/generic launch, or known-competition milestone) rather than a fresh unexpected cut to forward revenue/EPS, THEN do NOT forecast next-day Down; lean Neutral/Up, because gradual, years-anticipated erosion is already embedded in estimates and the arrival of the known date — especially when paired with a litigation settlement that removes uncertainty — clears an overhang rather than delivering a new negative surprise (scope: healthcare/regulated sectors, any regime, patent-cliff/known-competition catalyst)
§
33. IF a pick's bearish thesis rests on a company's VOLUNTARY/strategic price cut aimed at expanding demand or access (self-initiated, not a regulator- or payer-mandated concession) AND the same event pool contains fresh cutoff-dated positive catalysts (price-target hikes, record volume/prescription data, or a label/product expansion) THEN do NOT forecast next-day Down; lean Neutral, because a self-chosen price-for-volume trade is ambiguous rather than value-destructive and the concurrent hard positives offset it, so an already-declined tape stalls/mean-reverts instead of continuing lower (scope: healthcare/consumer, any regime, voluntary-pricing + analyst-action catalyst)
§
34. IF a stock is in a sharp multi-session decline but its cutoff-dated event pool is net-positive (multiple PT hikes, record operational metrics, regulatory approvals) with NO fresh hard negative catalyst dated at cutoff THEN do NOT extrapolate the downtrend into next-day Down; lean Neutral because oversold price action against confirming positive catalysts resolves as consolidation/mean-reversion rather than continuation (scope: market-wide, any regime, mixed tape-vs-catalyst)
§
35. IF a pick's fresh bullish catalyst grants new market access or removes an export/legal overhang BUT is explicitly conditioned on a mandated revenue-share, fee, or margin/pricing concession on the core product THEN do NOT forecast next-day continuation Up; lean Neutral, because the market nets the guaranteed economics haircut against the speculative volume upside and treats the "conditional win" as ambiguous rather than a clean forward-earnings upgrade (scope: market-wide, any regime, regulatory/access-deal-with-concession catalyst)
§
36. IF a pick's directional catalysts are dated at the cutoff but are opinion/narrative/positioning in nature (rating changes, executive or political rhetoric, competing-bid headlines) AND the tape has ALREADY made a large multi-session move in that direction such that the underlying thesis is widely known, THEN do NOT treat cutoff-dating as proof of freshness; lean Neutral, because a catalyst newly dated is not the same as newly priced — an already-stretched tape has absorbed the known narrative and the exhausted informed side mean-reverts rather than extending (scope: market-wide, any regime, narrative/rating/positioning catalyst on a moved tape)
§
37. IF a stock's catalyst session delivers BOTH a beat-and-raise into a skeptical/bearish-positioned crowd AND a concurrent fresh structural catalyst that opens a new distribution channel or addressable market (a platform integration exposing the product to a large new user base, a new monetization stream) THEN stack the two mechanisms and raise conviction toward a stronger-magnitude continuation Up, because the skepticism-reset short-covering (positioning repricing) and the TAM/channel expansion (forward-earnings-ceiling repricing) are independent unpriced drivers that compound rather than overlap, extending the re-rating across multiple sessions (scope: technology/semiconductors, any regime, earnings + structural-demand catalyst)
§
38. NO_NEW_RULE
§
39. IF a pick's bullish next-day thesis rests on a sector-rotation inflow that is itself triggered by ANOTHER basket name's same-session selloff (reactive "flight-to-value/defensive" flow), AND the pick has already jumped to a window high on that same session, THEN downgrade toward Neutral rather than predicting continuation Up, because reactive rebalancing flow is a one-day spasm that terminates once the source name's selloff pauses and is already embedded in the current close, leaving no fresh idiosyncratic buyer for the next session (scope: market-wide, risk-off/rotation regime, cross-asset rotation-flow catalyst)
§
40. IF a pick's only fresh cutoff-dated catalysts are analyst initiations/price-target hikes reinforcing an already-known bull thesis AND its confirming macro driver is a shared basket-wide rotation narrative rather than a company-specific operational surprise, THEN cap conviction at Neutral even when the tape breaks out on the cutoff session, because stacking two non-idiosyncratic derivative catalysts (opinion + flow) still fails to prove a durable marginal buyer and the breakout consolidates once both shared narratives are absorbed (scope: industrials/value sectors, momentum/late-cycle regime, analyst-action + rotation-flow catalyst)
§
41. IF a pick's bearish next-day thesis rests on a macro/commodity price shock that has ALREADY produced a sharp down-close on the cutoff session WHILE the same stock's event pool contains fresh company-specific POSITIVE catalysts (raised multi-year plan, favorable rotation, upgraded outlook), THEN do NOT forecast next-day Down; lean Neutral/Up, because the one-session macro drop is beta that reverts once selling pauses and the net-positive idiosyncratic drivers reassert as the marginal buyer returns (scope: energy/commodity-linked sectors, any regime, macro price-shock + company-specific catalyst)
§
42. IF a pick's bearish thesis layers a fresh opinion-based downgrade (analyst rating/PT cut) onto a stock whose underlying report was actually a beat AND whose tape has already fallen sharply into the cutoff, THEN do NOT extrapolate next-day Down; lean Neutral, because a derivative opinion catalyst on an already-oversold beat-report tape supplies no new cash-flow negative and the informed sellers are exhausted, producing consolidation/mean-reversion rather than continuation lower (scope: market-wide, any regime, analyst-action on a beat-report + declining tape)
§
43. IF a beat-and-raise pick's event pool shows a cluster of analyst rating UPGRADES and price-target hikes dated AFTER the earnings print (on or into the cutoff session) rather than before it, THEN override the priced-in/parabolic-fade instinct and lean continuation Up, because post-print upgrades are the sell-side catching up to a genuinely new fundamental fact and unlock a fresh wave of mandate-constrained institutional buyers over subsequent sessions, unlike pre-event hikes that merely front-run and price in the outcome (scope: technology/semiconductors, momentum/late-cycle regime, earnings + analyst-action)
§
44. IF a pick's beat-and-raise session simultaneously reports that forward capacity/product is explicitly SOLD OUT or fully booked across future periods while the company is raising capex to expand supply, THEN do NOT fade the gap as spent; lean continuation Up, because sold-out forward supply removes demand uncertainty and converts the beat into a durable multi-quarter earnings-visibility re-rate that the market prices across multiple sessions rather than a one-session catalyst that reverts (scope: technology/semiconductors, momentum/late-cycle regime, earnings + structural-demand catalyst)
§
45. IF a beat-and-raise pick's post-print sessions have ALREADY delivered a multi-session parabolic acceleration into the cutoff (several consecutive higher closes AFTER the report, not into it) such that the post-print upgrade cluster and sold-out/structural narrative have had multiple sessions to be absorbed, THEN do NOT treat the accelerating tape as fresh continuation fuel (do not apply the Rule 43/44/10 continuation override); lean Neutral, because the durable re-rate has been front-loaded into the post-print run and a parabolic close into cutoff marks late-stage absorption where the marginal buyer is thinning rather than arriving (scope: technology/semiconductors, momentum/late-cycle regime, earnings + analyst-action)
§
46. IF a pick's bullish thesis derives its hard catalyst from a PEER company's earnings/capex (spillover beneficiary) while the pick's own fresh cutoff-dated catalysts are exclusively analyst PT hikes and its tape has already run parabolically on that borrowed narrative, THEN downgrade toward Neutral rather than predicting continuation Up, because a stock lacking its own idiosyncratic operational surprise re-rates only as far as the peer's news carries it and consolidates once that spillover is absorbed, leaving no proven marginal buyer for the next session (scope: technology/semiconductors, momentum/late-cycle regime, peer-spillover + analyst-action catalyst)
§
47. IF a pick's accumulating demand-datastream catalyst (opening-weekend box office, launch-week or holiday-period sales) has a latest hard data point that UNDERSHOOTS its most directly comparable prior benchmark (a prior installment's debut, a prior season) AND the tape has already climbed steadily through the earlier fragments into a window high, THEN do NOT apply the Rule 29 continuation-Up override; lean Neutral/Down, because the market re-rates on the expectations gap of the freshest number rather than the celebratory milestone headlines, and a tape that already priced the earlier fragments reverts once the disappointing comparable is recognized (scope: consumer/media sectors, momentum/late-cycle regime, product-launch/demand-datastream catalyst)
§
48. IF a pick's event pool is dominated by celebratory milestone/record-absolute headlines (cumulative revenue crossing a round number, "biggest ever" tags) whose underlying operational surprise is either in-line or a miss versus expectations, AND the tape has already trended up across the window absorbing each headline, THEN downgrade next-day continuation toward Neutral, because milestone framing is backward-looking narrative that restates already-priced results rather than supplying a fresh forward-earnings upgrade, leaving no proven marginal buyer (scope: market-wide, momentum/late-cycle regime, milestone/record-absolute narrative catalyst)
§
49. IF the broad basket is in a sustained risk-on grind (the majority of large-cap names printing higher closes into the cutoff, no macro risk-off catalyst) AND a pick's bearish/Neutral thesis rests on single-name exhaustion, priced-in, or spent-catalyst logic, THEN do NOT fade the pick toward Down/Neutral; downgrade the conviction of the fade itself and lean with the tape, because a market-wide melt-up supplies fresh marginal buyers to every name and overrides idiosyncratic exhaustion tells until the broad bid breaks (scope: market-wide, risk-on/melt-up regime, any catalyst)
§
50. IF a pick's bearish thesis rests on a fresh hard negative catalyst that ALREADY produced its full down-move on the SAME catalyst session (the reported drop is dated to the catalyst) AND the tape has since flattened at window lows rather than continuing to fall, THEN do NOT forecast next-day Down; lean Neutral/Up, because a same-session-priced negative is absorbed rather than pending and a stabilized bottom invites oversold mean-reversion rather than fresh continuation lower (scope: market-wide, any regime, hard-negative catalyst)
§
51. IF a pick's bullish thesis rests on a single-name catalyst (validation stake, sold-out/structural re-rate, beaten-down reset) while the same cutoff sessions carry a fresh, explicitly-named risk-off/de-risking flow OUT of the pick's own cohort (tech/AI/semis) THEN downgrade the bullish pick toward Neutral/Down rather than predicting continuation Up, because a cohort-wide outflow supplies fresh marginal SELLERS to every name in the group and overrides idiosyncratic bull tells until the outflow pauses — the mirror of a melt-up lifting all names (scope: technology/semiconductors, risk-off/rotation regime, single-name catalyst under cohort de-risking)
§
52. IF a beaten-down stock's fresh cutoff-dated "validation" catalyst is a strategic investor buying newly-issued shares in a private placement priced at or below the prevailing market price THEN do NOT treat it as a skepticism-reset Up catalyst (do not apply the Rule 2 override); lean Neutral/Down, because dilutive issuance below market is a per-share value transfer to the new holder and signals the company needed capital rather than a forward earnings/order upgrade, so the marginal reaction is repricing toward the placement level rather than short-covering continuation (scope: market-wide, any regime, strategic-investment/equity-placement catalyst)
§
53. IF a beaten-down stock's tape has already printed a sequence of consecutive higher closes off its window low into the cutoff (a confirmed reversal, not a single snapback bar) AND its cutoff-dated event pool is net-positive with fresh hard catalysts (PT hikes plus rotation inflows or an operational upgrade), THEN override the oversold-consolidation default and lean continuation Up, because the multi-session rising sequence proves the marginal buyer has already arrived and is absorbing supply, converting a mean-reversion bounce into a live re-rating trend (scope: market-wide, any regime, mixed tape-vs-catalyst + analyst-action)
§
54. IF a post-print analyst-upgrade-cluster pick (the Rule 43 continuation setup) ALSO carries concurrent fresh cutoff-dated structural catalysts that open a new product line or partner-driven addressable market (product launch, platform integration with a large partner) landing alongside the upgrade wave, THEN raise the magnitude of the continuation Up toward strong, because the analyst catch-up (positioning repricing) and the structural TAM catalyst (forward-earnings-ceiling repricing) are independent unpriced drivers that compound across sessions rather than overlapping (scope: technology/semiconductors, momentum/late-cycle regime, earnings + analyst-action + structural-demand catalyst)
§
55. IF a beat-and-raise pick carries a durable structural bull narrative (sold-out capacity, capex expansion, multi-quarter visibility) BUT its post-print tape has already run parabolically and is printing consecutive DOWN closes into the cutoff, THEN let the rolling-over price action override the durable-narrative continuation instinct and lean Neutral/Down, because narrative durability governs multi-quarter fundamentals not next-day direction, and a parabolic tape that has begun to close lower signals the marginal buyer has exhausted and profit-taking is repricing the front-loaded gains (scope: technology/semiconductors, momentum/late-cycle regime, earnings + structural-demand catalyst)
§
56. IF two of a batch's picks share the same structural setup (fresh post-print upgrade cluster + beat) but differ in tape shape — one consolidating/non-extended, the other parabolically extended into cutoff — THEN preferentially assign continuation-Up conviction to the non-extended tape and downgrade the extended one, because the identical catalyst re-rates the name with proven marginal buyers still arriving (consolidating tape) but has already been front-loaded and absorbed where the tape is parabolic, making the two setups directionally opposite despite reading as equally bullish on catalyst grounds (scope: market-wide, momentum/late-cycle regime, earnings + analyst-action)
§
57. IF a beat-and-raise pick's event pool simultaneously contains explicit evidence that named direct competitors are failing, yielding-out, or ceding capacity/share to the pick (rival yield problems, rivals cutting their own production targets, rivals locked out of a constrained input) THEN raise the MAGNITUDE of the continuation Up toward strong, because competitor forfeiture removes demand-substitution risk and hands the pick monopoly-like pricing/volume power — a durable forward-earnings driver that the market re-rates across multiple sessions rather than absorbing in one close (scope: technology/semiconductors, momentum/late-cycle regime, earnings + structural-demand/competitive-displacement catalyst)
§
58. NO_NEW_RULE
§
59. # Comprehensive Analysis

Let me work through the basket, isolating names with clean, high-conviction structural signals aligned to the durable rules.

## Macro Context
There's a strong crosscurrent: on 2026-03-25, a U.S. 15-point Iran peace plan drove Brent down ~5% and sparked a broad relief rally (per COST, AMAT, CRM references). Yet AAPL/BAC references note Iran *rejected* a ceasefire keeping oil elevated. The signals are mixed on the macro reversal — Rule 63 warns against treating a one-day relief-reversal as continuation fuel. I'll be cautious with pure macro-beta picks.

## Candidates with Clean Signals

**MRK (Merck)** — Fresh cutoff-dated catalyst is Merck as the **ACQUIRER** paying $53/share all-cash (~$6.7B) for Terns. Rule 19 directly applies: the acquirer outlays cash and assumes integration risk; the premium accrues to the target. Acquirer reaction is muted-to-flat. However, MRK's tape is actually rising into cutoff (116.4→119.4, a strong +2.6% up-close on 3/25). That last-bar jump is the acquisition reaction already. Rule 19 says lean Neutral, but the tape already gapped. I'll classify **Neutral** — the deal is not an earnings surprise and the pop is spent.

**CVX (Chevron)** — Fresh cutoff-dated catalyst: Raymond James raised PT from $187→$238 and nearly doubled 2026 EPS estimate ($7.50→$14.00). Tape is in a clean multi-session uptrend (188→204). This is a fresh, hard, dramatic analyst revision on a confirmed rising tape. But oil just dropped 5% on the peace plan (Rule 3/41 tension). The stock closed slightly down on 3/25 (204.9→203.3). Given oil weakness pressures energy next day, but the massive EPS revision provides idiosyncratic support — this is genuinely mixed. I'll lean **Neutral**.

**AMD** — Fresh catalysts: CPU price hikes 10-15% (April), plus MI355 order talks. Tape is parabolic into cutoff (205.4→220.3, +7.3% single bar on 3/25). Rule 5/64: an outsized parabolic single-bar advance driven by a catalyst already embedded invites profit-taking/mean-reversion. The price-hike news is positive but the parabolic exhaustion bar is a fade tell. Lean **Down/Neutral**. I'll go **Down** given the extreme single-session gap.

**MU (Micron)** — TurboQuant (Google memory compression) is a genuine negative for memory demand narrative. Tape is in sharp decline (461→381, multi-session breakdown). This is a clean continuation-Down setup — fresh bearish catalyst + confirming downtrend. **Down**.
